CHAIRMAN’S STATEMENT
主席報告

BUSINESS REVIEW                                                                       業務回顧
On behalf of the board (“Board”) of directors (“Directors”), I am delighted           本人謹代表董事會，欣然向各位股東公佈，建
to report to our shareholders (“Shareholders”) that Kingboard Laminates               滔積層板控股有限公司（「公司」）及其附屬公司
Holdings Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (the “Group”) delivered        （「集團」）於截至二零一八年十二月三十一日止
solid results for the financial year ended 31 December 2018. During the year,         財政年度取得理想業績。二零一八年，環球貿
global trade disputes brought greater uncertainty to the electronics industry.        易爭端為電子行業銷售帶來較大不確定性，加
This, coupled with the slowed growth in the demand for auto mobile and                上國內汽車及家電等產品消費增速放緩，覆銅
home appliances in China, had posed downward pressure on demand in                    面板行業需求相對回落。但集團憑藉完善的覆
the laminates industry. Nevertheless, the Group was able to capitalise on its         銅面板垂直供應產業鏈、高效的成本控制及多
vertical laminates supply chain, effective cost control and diversified business      元化的業務組合，收入持續增長，整體盈利能
portfolio to sustain revenue growth. The Group’s overall profitability has            力亦繼續領跑同業。
continuously outperformed its peers.

PERFORMANCE                                                                        業務表現
The management team introduced new sales initiatives in tune with the              二零一八年，管理團隊及時根據市場狀況作
market conditions, and expedited the upgrade of the product mix, thus              出相應提振銷售的方案，並加速產品組合升
resulting in an increase in the average selling price (“ASP”) during 2018.         級，推動產品平均單價上揚。但年內覆銅面
However, in view of the overall weakened demand for laminates, the division        板市場需求較疲弱，加上銅及化工等原材料
was unable to fully shift the cost increases of the materials, such as copper      價格高漲，覆銅面板部門難以將高企的成本
and chemicals to customers. Laminates sales showed a modest decline                全數轉嫁予客戶。覆銅面板銷售量亦輕微回
with the average monthly shipment volume standing at 9.64 million square           落，集團每月平均出貨量為九百六十四萬
metres. The laminates division’s segment revenue rose 2% to HK$16,747.3            平 方 米 。 覆 銅 面 板 部 門 營 業 額 增 加 2% ， 至
million. Earnings before interest, tax, depreciation and amortisation (“EBITDA”)   一百六十七億四千七百三十萬港元，部門未
declined 30% to HK$3,378.9 million.                                                扣除利息、稅項、折舊及攤銷前之利潤下降
                                                                                   30%，至三十三億七千八百九十萬港元。

year. The sales of Kunshan Development Zone Kingboard Yu Garden Phase              山開發區建滔裕花園二期、花橋建滔裕花園四

PROSPECTS                                                                         前景
Stepping into 2019, investments in the industrial chain of 5G mobile networks     踏入二零一九年，5G網絡產業鏈投資已見啟
and the introduction of consumption stimulus policies in China are expected       動，國內刺激消費政策亦相繼推出，預期覆銅
to drive a rebound in laminates demand. Prices of laminates are already           面板市場需求將反彈回升。目前，覆銅面板產
on the upward track, while costs have remained relatively stable. This is         品價格開始上行，成本則相對平穩，有利於部
favourable for the division to expand its profit margin. Coping with market       門利潤率擴張。因應現時市場要求提升，部門

           In the current year, the Group has applied HKFRS 9 “Financial                 於本年度，本集團已應用香港財
           Instruments” and the related consequential amendments                         務申報準則第9號「金融工具」及
           to other HKFRSs. HKFRS 9 introduces new requirements                          相應的其他香港財務申報準則相
           for 1) the classification and measurement of financial assets                 關修訂。香港財務申報準則第9
           and financial liabilities, 2) expected credit losses (“ECL”) for              號引入新要求：1)金融資產及金
           financial assets and 3) general hedge accounting.                             融負債的分類及計量、2)金融資
                                                                                         產的預期信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧
                                                                                         損」）及3)一般對沖會計法。

           The Group has applied HKFRS 9 in accordance with the                          本集團已根據香港財務申報準則
           transition provisions set out in HKFRS 9, i.e. applied the                    第9號所載的過渡條文應用香港
           classification and measurement requirements (including                        財務申報準則第9號，即將分類
           impairment under ECL model) retrospectively to instruments                    及計量規定（包括預期信貸虧損

     TO HONG KONG FINANCIAL REPORTING                                                   港財務申報準則（「香港
     STANDARDS (“HKFRSs”) (continued)                                                   財務申報準則」）
                                                                                               （續）

     New and amendments to HKFRSs in issue but not yet                                  已頒佈但尚未生效的新增及經修
     effective (continued)                                                              訂香港財務申報準則（續）
     Except for the new and amendments to HKFRSs and the interpretation                 除以下新增及經修訂香港財務申報準則
     mentioned below, the Directors anticipate that the application of all              及詮釋外，董事預計應用所有其他新增
     other new and amendments to HKFRSs will have no material impact                    及經修訂香港財務申報準則對本集團於
     on the consolidated financial statements in the foreseeable future.                可見將來的綜合財務報表並無重大影
                                                                                        響。

     HKFRS 16 “Leases”                                                                  香 港 財 務 申 報 準 則 第 16 號 「 租
                                                                                        賃」
     HKFRS 16 introduces a comprehensive model for the identification of                香港財務申報準則第16號為識別出租人
     lease arrangements and accounting treatments for both lessors and                  及承租人的租賃安排及會計處理引入一
     leases. HKFRS 16 will supersede HKAS 17 Leases and the related                     個綜合模式。當香港財務申報準則第16
     interpretations when it becomes effective.                                         號生效時，將取代香港會計準則第17號
                                                                                        「租賃」及相關的詮釋。

     HKFRS 16 distinguishes leases and service contracts on the basis of                香港財務申報準則第16號根據所識別資
     whether an identified asset is controlled by a customer.                           產是否由客戶控制來區分租賃及服務合
                                                                                        約。

     Distinctions of operating leases and finance leases are removed for                除短期租賃及低值資產租賃外，經營及
     lessee accounting, and is replaced by a model where a right-of-use                 融資租賃的差異自承租人會計處理中移
     asset and a corresponding liability have to be recognised for all leases           除，並由承租人須就所有租賃確認使用
     by lessees, except for short-term leases and leases of low value                   權資產及相應負債的模式替代。
     assets.

     The right-of-use asset is initially measured at cost and subsequently              使用權資產初步按成本計量，隨後以成
     measured at cost (subject to certain exceptions) less accumulated                  本（惟若干例外情況除外）減累計折舊及
     depreciation and impairment losses, adjusted for any remeasurement                 減值虧損計量，並就租賃負債的任何重
     of the lease liability. The lease liability is initially measured at the           新計量而作出調整。租賃負債初步按租
     present value of the lease payments that are not paid at that date.                賃款項（非當日支付）之現值計量。隨
     Subsequently, the lease liability is adjusted for interest and lease               後，租賃負債會就（其中包括）利息及租
     payments, as well as the impact of lease modifications, amongst                    賃款項以及租賃修訂的影響作出調整。
     others. For the classification of cash flows, the Group currently                  就現金流量分類而言，本集團目前將預
     presents upfront prepaid lease payments as investing cash flows                    付租賃款項呈列為與自用租賃土地及分
     in relation to leasehold lands for own use and those classified as                 類為投資物業的租賃土地有關的投資現
     investment properties while other operating lease payments are                     金流量，其他經營租賃款項則呈列為經
     presented as operating cash flows. Upon application of HKFRS 16,                   營現金流量。本集團應用香港財務申報
     lease payments in relation to lease liability will be allocated into a             準則第16號時，有關租賃負債的租賃款
     principal and an interest portion which will be presented as financing             項將分配至本金及利息部分，並以融資
     cash flows by the Group.                                                           現金流量呈列。

3.   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                             3.   主要會計政策
     The consolidated financial statements have been prepared in                      綜合財務報表乃按香港會計師公會頒佈
     accordance with HKFRSs issued by the HKICPA. In addition, the                    的香港財務申報準則編製。此外，綜合
     consolidated financial statements include applicable disclosures                 財務報表載有聯交所證券上市規則及香
     required by the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on the Stock           港公司條例之適用披露規定。
     Exchange and by the Hong Kong Companies Ordinance.

     The consolidated financial statements have been prepared on the                  綜合財務報表乃按歷史成本基準編製，
     historical cost basis except for investment properties and certain               惟如下文所載之會計政策所闡述，投資
     financial instruments, that are measured at fair values at the end of            物業及若干金融工具則於各報告期間結
     each reporting period, as explained in the accounting policies set out           束當日按公平值計量。
     below.

     Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the consideration        歷史成本一般按為換取貨品及服務而付
     given in exchange for goods and services.                                        出的代價的公平值計算。

     Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset or               公平值是於計量日期市場參與者間於有
     paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between market            秩序交易中出售資產所收取或轉讓負債
     participants at the measurement date, regardless of whether that                 須支付之價格，而不論該價格為可直接
     price is directly observable or estimated using another valuation                觀察取得或可使用其他估值方法估計。
     technique. In estimating the fair value of an asset or a liability, the          於估計資產或負債之公平值時，本集團
     Group takes into account the characteristics of the asset or liability           會考慮該等市場參與者於計量日期對資
     if market participants would take those characteristics into account             產或負債定價時所考慮之資產或負債之
     when pricing the asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair value          特點。於該等綜合財務報表中作計量
     for measurement and/or disclosure purposes in these consolidated                 及╱或披露用途之公平值乃按此基準釐
     financial statements is determined on such basis, except for share-              定，惟以下各項除外：屬於香港財務申
     based payment transactions that are within the scope of HKFRS                    報準則第2號「以股份形式付款」範圍內
     2 “Share-based payment”, leasing transactions that are within the                之以股份形式付款交易、屬於香港會計
     scope of HKAS 17 “Leases”, and measurements that have some                       準則第17號「租賃」範圍內之租賃交易，
     similarities to fair value but are not fair value, such as net realisable        以及與公平值存在某些相似之處但並非
     value in HKAS 2 “Inventories” or value in use in HKAS 36 “Impairment             公平值之項目計量，例如香港會計準則
     of assets”.                                                                      第2號「存貨」之可變現淨值或香港會計
                                                                                      準則第36號「資產減值」之使用價值。

     A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into account             非金融資產公平值之計量則參考市場參
     a market participant’s ability to generate economic benefits by using            與者可從使用該資產得到之最高及最佳
     the asset in its highest and best use or by selling it to another market         效用，或把該資產售予另一可從使用該
     participant that would use the asset in its highest and best use.                資產得到最高及最佳效用之市場參與者
                                                                                      所產生之經濟效益。

     For financial instruments and investment properties which are                    就其後期間使用不可觀察輸入數據按公
     transacted at fair value and a valuation technique that unobservable             平值計量的金融工具及投資物業，估值
     inputs is to be used to measure fair values in subsequent periods,               方法已經校準，使估值方法的結果與交
     the valuation technique is calibrated so that at initial recognition the         易價格相等。
     results of the valuation technique equal the transaction price.

     (continued)

     In addition, for financial reporting purposes, fair value measurements               此外，就財務報告而言，公平值計量分
     are categorised into Level 1, 2 or 3 based on the degree to which                    為第一級別、第二級別或第三級別，此
     the inputs to the fair value measurements are observable and the                     等級別之劃分乃根據公平值計量輸入數
     significance of the inputs to the fair value measurement in its entirety,            據的可觀察程度及該輸入數據對公平值
     which are described as follows:                                                      計量的整體重要性，概述如下：

     •       Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets              •    第一級別輸入數據指該實體於計
             for identical assets or liabilities that the entity can access at                 量日期由活躍市場上相同資產或
             the measurement date;                                                             負債獲得的報價（未經調整）；

     •       Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted prices included                 •    第二級別輸入數據指除第一級別
             within Level 1, that are observable for the asset or liability,                   所包含之報價以外，資產或負債
             either directly or indirectly; and                                                可直接或間接觀察得出的數據；
                                                                                               及

     •       Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset or liability.           •    第三級別輸入數據指資產或負債
                                                                                               的不可觀察數據。

     The principal accounting policies are set out below.                                 主要會計政策載列如下。

     Basis of consolidation                                                               綜合賬目基準
     The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial                      綜合財務報表包括本公司及由本公司及
     statements of the Company and entities controlled by the Company                     其附屬公司控制之實體之財務報表。當
     and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved when the Company:                          本公司符合以下情況，即取得控制權：

     •       has power over the investee;                                                 •    有權控制被投資方；

     •       is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its                      •    因其參與被投資方業務而對可變
             involvement with the investee; and                                                回報承擔風險或享有權利；及

     •       has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.                      •    有能力以其權力影響其回報。

     The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if facts                 倘有事實及情況顯示上列三項控制權條
     and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one or more of                  件之其中一項或多項有變，本集團會重
     the three elements of control listed above.                                          新評估其是否控制被投資方。

     Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group obtains control                  當本集團取得附屬公司之控制權，便將
     over the subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses control of the                   該附屬公司綜合入賬；當本集團失去附
     subsidiary. Specifically, income and expenses of a subsidiary acquired               屬公司之控制權，便停止將該附屬公司
     or disposed of during the year are included in the consolidated                      綜合入賬。具體而言，年內收購或出售
     statement of profit or loss from the date the Group gains control until              之附屬公司之收入及支出會於本集團取
     the date when the Group ceases to control the subsidiary.                            得控制權當日起計入綜合損益表，直至
                                                                                          本集團對該附屬公司之控制權終止當日
                                                                                          為止。

     Business combinations                                                                業務合併

     and the equity interests issued by the Group in exchange for control                 期的公平值總和。與收購事項有關的成
     of the acquiree. Acquisition-related costs are generally recognised in               本通常於產生時在損益確認。
     profit or loss as incurred.

     HKFRS 15 introduces a 5-step approach when recognising revenue:                    香港財務申報準則第15號引入收益確認
                                                                                        的五個步驟：

     •      Step 1: Identify the contract(s) with a customer                            •    第1步：確定與客戶的合約

     •      Step 2: Identify the performance obligations in the contract                •    第2步：確定合約內的履約義務

     •      Step 3: Determine the transaction price                                     •    第3步：釐定交易價格

     •      Step 4: Allocate the transaction price to the performance                   •    第4步：將交易價格分配至合約
            obligations in the contract                                                      內的履約義務

     •      Step 5: Recognise revenue when (or as) the Group satisfies a                •    第5步：於本集團履行履約義務
            performance obligation                                                           時（或就此）確認收益

     Under HKFRS 15, the Group recognises revenue when (or as) a                         根據香港財務申報準則第15號，本集團
     performance obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the goods or            於履行履約義務時（或就此）確認收益，
     services underlying the particular performance obligation is transferred            即與特定履約義務相關的貨品或服務的
     to the customer.                                                                   「控制權」轉移予客戶時確認收益。

     A performance obligation represents a good or service (or a bundle                 履約責任指一項明確貨品或服務（或一
     of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of distinct goods or            批貨品或服務）或一系列大致相同之明
     services that are substantially the same.                                          確貨品或服務。

     Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised over time               倘符合下列其中一項標準，貨品或服務
     by reference to the progress towards complete satisfaction of the                  的控制權在一段時間內轉移，而我們會
     relevant performance obligation if one of the following criteria is met:           按一段時間內已完成相關履約責任的進
                                                                                        度確認收益：

     •      the customer simultaneously receives and consumes the                       •    客戶同時收取及耗用由本集團履
            benefits provided by the Group’s performance as the Group                        約所帶來的利益；
            performs;

     •      the Group’s performance creates and enhances an asset that                  •    本集團履約導致創建及提升於本
            the customer controls as the Group performs; or                                  集團履約時由客戶控制的資產；
                                                                                             或

     •      the Group’s performance does not create an asset with                       •    本集團履約並無產生對本集團有
            an alternative use to the Group and the Group has an                             替代用途的資產，且本集團可享
            enforceable right to payment for performance completed to                        有強制執行權，以收回至今已履
            date.                                                                            約部分的款項。

     In determining the transaction price, the Group adjusts the promised            釐定交易價時，倘協定之付款時間（明

      Impairment losses on tangible assets other than                                        有形資產之減值虧損（商譽除外
      goodwill (see the accounting policy in respect of                                     （請參閱有關商譽之會計政策））
      goodwill)
      At the end of the reporting period, the Group reviews the carrying                    於報告期間結束當日，本集團須審閱其
      amounts of its tangible assets to determine whether there is any                      有形資產之賬面值，以確定是否有任何
      indication that these assets have suffered an impairment loss. If any                 跡象顯示該等資產出現減值虧損。倘出
      such indication exists, the recoverable amount of the relevant asset                  現任何有關跡象，則估計有關資產可
      is estimated in order to determine the extent of the impairment loss, if              收回金額，以釐定減值虧損的幅度（如
      any.                                                                                  有）。

      The recoverable amount of tangible assets are estimated individually,                 有形及無形資產的可收回金額個別估
      when it is not possible to estimate the recoverable amount                            計，倘不能估計單一資產的可收回金
      individually, the Group estimates the recoverable amount of the cash-                 額，則本集團會估計其資產所屬現金產
      generating unit to which the asset belongs. When a reasonable and                     生單位的可收回金額。於可識別合理和
      consistent basis of allocation can be identified, corporate assets are                一貫分配基準的情況下，企業資產亦會
      also allocated to individual cash-generating units, or otherwise they                 被分配到個別的現金產生單位，否則或
      are allocated to the smallest group of cash-generating units for which                會被分配到可合理地及按一貫分配基準
      a reasonable and consistent allocation basis can be identified.                       而識別的最小的現金產生單位組別中。

      Recoverable amount is the higher of fair value less costs of disposal                 可收回金額為公平值減出售成本或使用
      and value in use. In assessing value in use, the estimated future cash                價值兩者中之較高者。評估使用價值
      flows are discounted to their present value using a pre-tax discount                  時，估計未來現金流量乃使用稅前貼現
      rate that reflects current market assessments of the time value of                    率折現至其現值，該貼現率反映目前市
      money and the risks specific to the asset (or a CGU) for which the                    場對資金時間值之評估以及估計未來現
      estimates of future cash flows have not been adjusted.                                金流量未經調整之資產（或現金產生單
                                                                                            位）的獨有風險。

      If the recoverable amount of an asset (or a CGU) is estimated to                      倘某項資產（或現金產生單位）之可收回
      be less than its carrying amount, the carrying amount of the asset                    金額估計低於其賬面值，則該項資產
      (or a CGU) is reduced to its recoverable amount. In allocating the                    （或現金產生單位）之賬面值須削減至其
      impairment loss, the impairment loss is allocated first to reduce the                 可收回金額。於分配減值虧損時，首先

      in the unit. The carrying amount of an asset is not reduced below the                 產的賬面值分配至其他資產。資產賬面
      highest of its fair value less costs of disposal (if measurable), its value           值不得減少至低於其公平值減出售成本

      that would otherwise have been allocated to the asset is allocated pro                零之中的最高值。已另行分配至資產之
      rata to the other assets of the unit. An impairment loss is recognised                減值虧損數額按比例分配至該單位其他
      immediately in profit or loss.                                                        資產。減值虧損即時在損益中確認。

      Foreign currencies                                                                   外幣
      In preparing the financial statements of each individual group entity,               編製各個別集團實體之財務報表時，以
      transactions in currencies other than the entity’s functional currency               該實體之功能貨幣以外貨幣（外幣）進
      (foreign currencies) are recognised at the rates of exchange prevailing              行之交易乃按於交易日期當時之匯率確
      at the dates of the transactions. At the end of the reporting period,                認。於報告期間結束當日，以外幣列值
      monetary items denominated in foreign currencies are retranslated at                 之貨幣項目以該日之匯率重新匯兌。按
      the rates prevailing at that date. Non-monetary items carried at fair                公平值列賬且按外幣列值之非貨幣項目
      value that are denominated in foreign currencies are retranslated at                 乃按釐定公平值當日匯率重新匯兌。以
      the rates prevailing at the date when the fair value was determined.                 外幣按歷史成本計量之非貨幣項目不予
      Non-monetary items that are measured in terms of historical cost in a                重新匯兌。
      foreign currency are not retranslated.

      Exchange differences arising on the settlement of monetary items,                    因結算貨幣項目及重新換算貨幣項目之
      and on the retranslation of monetary items, are recognised in profit or              匯兌差額乃於產生期間內於損益確認。
      loss in the period in which they arise.

      For the purposes of presenting the consolidated financial statements,                就呈列綜合財務報表而言，本集團海外
      the assets and liabilities of the Group’s foreign operations are                     業務之資產及負債均按報告期間結束當
      translated into the presentation currency of the Group (i.e. HK$) using              日匯率匯兌為本集團之呈報貨幣（即港

      and expenses items are translated at the average exchange rates                      率匯兌。所產生匯兌差額（如有）於其他
      for the year. Exchange differences arising, if any, are recognised in                全面收益確認，並於權益以匯兌儲備名
      other comprehensive income and accumulated in equity under the                       目（歸入非控股權益（如適用））累計。
      heading of translation reserve (attributed to non-controlling interests as
      appropriate).


      Borrowing costs                                                                      借貸成本
      Borrowing costs directly attributable to the acquisition, construction               與收購、建造或生產合資格資產（需一
      or production of qualifying assets, which are assets that necessarily                段長時間方可作擬定用途或可供銷售的
      take a substantial period of time to get ready for their intended use                資產）直接有關之借貸成本均加至該等

      assets are substantially ready for their intended use or sale.                       擬定用途或可供銷售為止。

      in which they are incurred.                                                          中確認。

     Taxation                                                                            稅項
     Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently payable                  所得稅開支指現時應繳稅項及遞延稅項
     and deferred tax.                                                                   之總和。

     The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the year.                  現時應繳稅項乃按本年度應課稅溢利計
     Taxable profit differs from profit before taxation because of income or             算。基於其他年度應課稅或可扣稅的收
     expense that are taxable or deductible in other years and items that                入或開支項目，以及毋須課稅或扣稅項
     are never taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for current tax              目，因此應課稅溢利與除稅前溢利不
     is calculated using tax rates that have been enacted or substantively               同。本集團即期稅項負債乃按報告期間
     enacted by the end of the reporting period.                                         結束當日已頒佈或實際已頒佈之稅率計
                                                                                         算。

     Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences between the                     遞延稅項乃根據綜合財務報表資產及負
     carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the consolidated financial            債賬面值與計算應課稅溢利所採用相應
     statements and the corresponding tax bases used in the computation                  稅基間之臨時差額確認。遞延稅項負債
     of taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are generally recognised for            一般會就所有應課稅臨時差額確認，而
     all taxable temporary differences. Deferred tax assets are generally                遞延稅項資產一般在可能出現可利用臨
     recognised for all deductible temporary difference to the extent that it is         時差額扣稅之應課稅溢利時就所有可扣
     probable that taxable profits will be available against which deductible            減臨時差額確認。因業務合併以外交易
     temporary differences can be utilised. Such deferred tax assets and                 初步確認資產及負債且不影響應課稅溢
     liabilities are not recognised if the temporary difference arises from the          利亦不影響會計溢利而引致之臨時差
     initial recognition (other than in a business combination) of assets and            額，則不會確認該等遞延稅項資產及負
     liabilities in a transaction that affects neither the taxable profit nor the        債。此外，不會就因初步確認商譽而引
     accounting profit. In addition, deferred tax liabilities are not recognised         致之臨時差額確認遞延稅項負債。
     if the temporary difference arises from the initial recognition of
     goodwill.

     (v)      Measurement and recognition of ECL                                     (v) 預期信貸虧損的計量及確認
              The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability of                 預期信貸虧損的計量取決於違約
              default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the loss if             概率、違約損失率（即如存在違
              there is a default) and the exposure at default. The assessment            約時的損失程度）及違約風險。
              of the probability of default and loss given default is based              對違約概率及違約損失率的評估
              on historical data adjusted by forward-looking information.                基於歷史數據，並就前瞻性信息
              Estimation of ECL reflects an unbiased and probability-                    作出調整。預期信貸虧損的估計
              weighted amount that is determined with the respective risks               反映以發生相關違約的風險為權
              of default occurring as the weights.                                       重的無偏概率加權金額。

            Generally, the ECL is the difference between all contractual                一般而言，預期信貸虧損為本集
            cash flows that are due to the Group in accordance with                     團按初始確認時釐定的實際利率
            the contract and the cash flows that the Group expects to                   貼現，按照合約應收到的所有合
            receive, discounted at the effective interest rate determined at            約現金流量與本集團預計收到的
            initial recognition.                                                        所有現金流量之間的差額。

            Where ECL is measured on a collective basis, the financial                  倘預期信貸虧損按集體基準計
            instruments are grouped on the basis below:                                 量，則金融工具按以下基準歸
                                                                                        類：

            •       Nature of financial instruments (i.e. the Group’s trade             •   金融工具的性質（即本集
                    receivables, debt instruments at FVTOCI, amounts                        團貿易應收賬款，按公
                    due from fellow subsidiaries, bills receivables, bank                   平值計入其他全面收益
                    balances and other receivables, are each assessed as                    之債務工具，應收同系
                    separate groups);                                                       附屬公司款項，應收票
                                                                                            據，銀行結餘及其他應
                                                                                            收賬款按個別組別進行
                                                                                            評核）；

            •       Past-due status;                                                    •   逾期狀況；

            •       Nature, size and industry of debtors; and                           •   債務人的性質、規模及
                                                                                            行業；及

            •       External credit ratings where available.                            •   外部信貸評級（如有）
                                                                                                     。

            The grouping is regularly reviewed by management to ensure                  歸類工作經管理層定期檢討，以
            the constituents of each group continue to share similar credit             確保各組別成分繼續分擔類似信
            risk characteristics.                                                       貸風險特性。

            Interest income is calculated based on the gross carrying                   利息收入按金融資產的賬面總值
            amount of the financial asset unless the financial asset is                 計算，除非金融資產出現信貸減
            credit impaired, in which case interest income is calculated                值，在這種情況下，利息收入按
            based on amortised cost of the financial asset.                             金融資產的攤銷成本計算。

      For all other financial assets, objective evidence of impairment could                就所有其他金融資產而言，減值的客觀

      difficulties.

      Objective evidence of impairment for a portfolio of receivables could                 應收賬款組合出現減值的客觀證據包括
      include the Group’s past experience of collecting payments, an                        本集團過往收款經驗、組合內逾期超過
      increase in the number of delayed payments in the portfolio past the                  信貸期之還款數目上升、國家或地區經
      credit period and observable changes in national or local economic                    濟狀況出現明顯變動導致應收賬款未能
      conditions that correlate with default on receivables.                                償還。

      For financial assets carried at amortised cost, the amount of the                     就按攤銷成本計值的金融資產而言，減
      impairment loss recognised is the difference between the asset’s                      值虧損金額確認為資產的賬面值與估計
      carrying amount and the present value of the estimated future cash                    未來現金流量的現值（以金融資產的原
      flows discounted at the financial asset’s original effective interest rate.           始實際利率折現）間的差額。

      For financial assets carried at cost, the amount of the impairment loss               就按成本計值的金融資產而言，減值虧
      is measured as the difference between the asset’s carrying amount                     損的金額以資產的賬面值與估計未來現
      and the present value of the estimated future cash flows discounted                   金流量的現值（以類似金融資產的當前
      at the current market rate of return for a similar financial asset. Such              市場回報率折現）間的差額計量。該等
      impairment loss will not be reversed in subsequent periods.                           減值虧損不會於其後期間撥回。

      The carrying amount of the financial asset is reduced by the                          所有金融資產的減值虧損會直接於金融
      impairment loss directly for all financial assets with the exception of               資產的賬面值中作出扣減，惟貿易應收

      of the allowance account are recognised in profit or loss. When a                     中確認。當貿易應收賬款被視為不可收
      trade receivable is considered uncollectible, it is written off against               回時，其將於撥備賬內撇銷。於其後重
      the allowance account. Subsequent recoveries of amounts previously                    新收取的先前撇銷的款項將計入損益。
      written off are credited to profit or loss.

     Share-based payments                                                             以股份形式付款

6.   SEGMENT INFORMATION                                                          6.   分部資料
     HKFRS 8 “Operating Segments” requires operating segments to be                    香港財務申報準則第8號「經營分部」要
     identified on the basis of internal reports about components of the               求以集團之主要營運決策者（「主要營運
     Group that are regularly reviewed by Chief Operating Decision Maker               決策者」）在對分部作資源分配及評估其
     (“CODM”) in order to allocate resources to segments and to assess                 表現上所定期審閱的有關集團不同部門
     their performance. Specifically, the Group’s operating and reportable             之內部報告作為確定經營分部之基準。
     segments under HKFRS 8 are organised into three main operating                    具體而言，在香港財務申報準則第8號
     divisions – (i) manufacturing and sale of laminates; (ii) properties; and         下，本集團之經營及報告分部分為三
     (iii) investments. The management aggregated the sales of properties,             個主要經營分部－(i)製造及銷售覆銅面
     rental and hotel income business into one reportable segment                      板、(ii)物業及(iii)投資。管理層將房地產
     because the financial performance of these businesses are affected                銷售、租金及酒店收入業務同列於一個
     by changes in the property market. No operating segments identified               報告分部中，因為這兩項業務的財務表
     by the CODM have been aggregated in arriving at the reportable                    現皆取決於房地產市場的變化。在達致
     segments of the Group.                                                            本集團報告分部時，並無綜合主要營運
                                                                                       決策者所識別的經營分部。

     been levied at progressive rates ranging from 30% to 60% on the                    （根據物業銷售收入扣去指定直接成本）
     appreciation of land value, represented by the excess of sales                     以累進稅率30%至60%基準繳交土地增
     proceeds of properties over prescribed direct costs. Prescribed                    值稅。指定直接成本界定為土地成本，
     direct costs are defined to include costs of land, development and                 發展及建築成本，及其他關於房產發展

     development. According to the State Administration of Taxation’s                   告，銷售物業時應暫繳土地增值稅，到
     official circulars, LAT shall be payable provisionally upon sales of               房產發展完成後才最終確認所得收益。
     the properties, followed by final ascertainment of the gain at the
     completion of the properties development.

     using the fair value model and are classified and accounted for as                        公平值模式計量，並分類及列作投資物
     investment properties. In estimating the fair value of the properties,                    業。於估計物業之公平值時，物業之最
     the highest and best use of the properties is their current use. There                    高及最佳用途為目前用途。於過往年度
     has been no change from the valuation technique used in prior years.                      所用之估值技術並無變動。

34. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                    34. 資本風險管理
     The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the Group will     本集團管理其資本以確保通過優化債務
     be able to continue as a going concern while maximising the return to       與股本結存為權益持有人爭取最高回
     stakeholders through the optimisation of the debt and equity balance.       報，使本集團旗下實體能夠持續經營。
     The Group’s overall strategy remains unchanged from prior year.             本集團之整體策略與過往年度相同。

     The capital structure of the Group consists of bank borrowings, net         誠如綜合權益變動表所披露，本集團之
     of cash and cash equivalents and equity attributable to owners of           資本結構包括銀行借貸（扣除現金及現
     the Company, comprising issued share capital, other reserves and            金等值項目）及本公司持有人應佔權益
     retained profits as disclosed in the consolidated statement of changes      （包括已發行股本、其他儲備及保留溢
     in equity.                                                                  利）。

     The Directors review the capital structure on a semi-annual basis.          董事每半年審閱資本結構。作為審閱一
     As part of this review, the Directors consider the cost of capital and      部分，董事將考慮資本成本與各類資本
     the risks associated with each class of capital. In the opinion of the      的風險。董事認為，本集團將通過支付
     Directors, the Group will balance its overall capital structure through     股息、發行新股、購回股份、發行新債
     the payment of dividends, new share issues and share buy-backs as           或者贖回現有債項，以平衡整體資本結
     well as the issue of new debt or the redemption of existing debt.           構。

           The Group only invests in debt securities with low credit risk.             本集團只投資低信貸風險的債務
           The Group’s debt instruments at FVTOCI mainly comprise                      證券。本集團按公平值計入其他
           listed bonds that are graded in the high investment grade                   全面收益的債務工具主要由按照
           as per globally understood definitions and therefore are                    全球理解的釋義獲得高投資評級
